\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|R_{1}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{2}n^{3/2}}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right).\end{split}

(E.4)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{2,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

Here,
X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta
and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\left[\frac{1}{2/n}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\alpha}\right)^{2}-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\log\left(\frac{\alpha+\beta}{2\beta}-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{2\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}\left(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta\right)\left(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta\right)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=:\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}+R_{2}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta),\end{split}

(E.5)

where by a direct evaluation and Lemma
B.4
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|R_{2}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\left[|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|+|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.\end{split}

(E.6)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{3,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

Now we deal with
X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}
. Here,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt=\left[-\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)n(\theta-\theta^{\prime})\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\alpha}\right)\right.\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\left.-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{2}n(\theta-\theta^{\prime})^{2}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}.\end{split}

(E.7)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{4,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

For
X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta
, we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\left[\frac{1}{2/n}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\alpha}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\beta}\right)^{2}-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{I_{4,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\log\left(\frac{\alpha+\beta}{2\beta}-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{2\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}\left(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta\right)\left(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta\right)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{4,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}.\end{split}

(E.8)

Consequently, by a direct evaluation and Lemma
B.4
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\left[|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|+|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.\end{split}

(E.9)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{5,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

We treat
\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta
and find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\left[\log\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)+\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2/n}\left(\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}-\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\right]\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt+\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)}{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})\right)}\right)\\
&\hskip 62.59596pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.\end{split}

(E.10)

Using a Taylor expansion, we get the alternative expression

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=-\left(\theta-\theta^{\prime}\right)\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\frac{2(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})}{(\alpha^{2}/n)\sqrt{2\pi\alpha^{2}/n}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.\end{split}

(E.11)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{6,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

Here,
\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}
and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\left[\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}-\frac{1}{2/n}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt-\log\left(\frac{\alpha+\beta}{2\alpha}+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{2\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}\left(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}\right)\left(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}\right)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}.\end{split}

(E.12)

Consequently, by a direct evaluation and Lemma
B.4
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\left[|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|+|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}|\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}.\end{split}

(E.13)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{7,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

In this case,
X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}
and a direct evaluation gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt=\left[\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)n(\theta-\theta^{\prime})\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\alpha}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}}{\beta}\right)\right.\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\left.-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{2}n(\theta-\theta^{\prime})^{2}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}.\end{split}

(E.14)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{8,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

Here, we deal with
\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}
and get the expansion

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 2.84544pt=\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+\left[\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}-\frac{1}{2/n}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\alpha}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\beta}\right)^{2}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{I_{8,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt-\log\left(\frac{\alpha+\beta}{2\alpha}+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{2\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}\left(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}\right)\left(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}\right)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{8,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=:\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}+R_{8}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta),\end{split}

(E.15)

where by a direct evaluation and Lemma
B.4
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|R_{8}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\left[|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|+|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}|\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}.\end{split}

(E.16)

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{I_{9,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}}
.

In this case,
\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}
. As for the first case, we present both first and second order Taylor expansion. The first order expansion is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\left(\theta-\theta^{\prime}\right)\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\frac{2(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})}{(\beta^{2}/n)\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\end{split}

(E.17)

and the second order expansion by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\left(\theta-\theta^{\prime}\right)\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\frac{2(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\theta^{\prime}+X_{(k-1)/n})}{(\beta^{2}/n)\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\\
&\hskip 62.59596pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\theta^{\prime}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 34.14322pt-\left(\theta-\theta^{\prime}\right)^{2}\left[\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{4}{\beta^{2}/n}-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{4(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{\beta^{4}/n^{2}}\right]\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\frac{1}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt-\left(\theta-\theta^{\prime}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\right)^{2}\frac{4(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{[P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)]^{2}}\frac{1}{2\pi\beta^{6}/n^{3}}\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{\beta^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=:\left(\theta-\theta^{\prime}\right)\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\frac{2(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\theta^{\prime}+X_{(k-1)/n})}{(\beta^{2}/n)\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\theta^{\prime}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}+R_{9}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta).\end{split}

(E.18)

Using boundedness of
x\mapsto(1+x^{2})\exp(-x^{2}/4)
and that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)\sqrt{1/n}}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sqrt{2\pi}\left|\frac{\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)}{\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sqrt{2\pi}\left|\frac{1}{\exp\left(\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{(k/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})\right)+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}}\right|

can be bounded independently of
n
for
X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|R_{9}(k,\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{2}n^{3/2}}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right).\end{split}

(E.19)

F
Proofs of the results of Section
3

In this section, we give the proofs of Proposition
3.1
and
3.4
in the respective order.

We give a complete description for the case
\theta\geq 0
. The case
\theta<0
can then be dealt with analogously. By linearity of conditional expectation,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sum_{j=1}^{9}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{j,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

In what follows, we evaluate each of the cases
j=1,\dots,9
on the right-hand side seperately. It will turn out that the first-order terms in the expansions (
E.1
)-(
E.19
) for
j\in\{1,2,3,7,8,9\}
contribute to
-|\theta|F_{\alpha,\beta}\Lambda_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}((X_{(k-1)/n})_{1\leq k\leq\lfloor nt\rfloor})
, whereas
j=4,5,6
are absorbed in the remainder term
r_{n}(t,\theta)
. In what follows, we denote with
\xi_{k}
an intermediate point
0\leq\xi_{k}\leq\theta
that occurs in the Lagrange form of Taylor’s theorem and may vary from line to line.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=1}
.

By the identity (
E.2
) (with
\theta^{\prime}=0
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\theta\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{4(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n})}{(2\alpha^{2}/n)\sqrt{2\pi\alpha^{2}/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.R_{1}(k,0,\theta)\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

Evaluation of the integral reveals

\displaystyle-\theta\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{4(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n})}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\alpha^{2}/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\theta\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi\alpha^{2}/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\theta\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi\alpha^{2}/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)+r_{n}^{1}(t,\theta),

where

r_{n}^{1}(t,\theta):=\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.R_{1}(k,0,\theta)\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

It remains to estimate the
L^{1}(\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}})
-norm of
\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}|r_{n}^{1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|
. With (
E.4
) and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)}{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})\right)}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\end{split}

(F.1)

for
X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}
, we find with the Gaussian tail inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.|R_{1}(k,0,\theta)|\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k})/\sqrt{2\alpha^{2}/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

Consequently, with Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor ns\rfloor}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta^{2}\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=2}
.

Using the decomposition (
E.5
) we find

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)dy+\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.R_{2}(k,0,\theta)\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\theta\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)+r_{n}^{2}(t,\theta),

where
r_{n}^{2}(t,\theta)=r_{n}^{2,1}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{2,2}(t,\theta)
with

\displaystyle r_{n}^{2,1}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.R_{2}(k,0,\theta)\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right],

\displaystyle r_{n}^{2,2}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\left(P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)-P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0};1/n)\right)dy.

In what follows, we are going to prove the uniform
L^{1}(\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}})
-bound on both
r_{n}^{2,1}(t,\theta),r_{n}^{2,2}(t,\theta)
seperately.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{2,1}}
.

From the bound (
E.6
) for
R_{2}(k,0,\theta)
, Lemma
B.1
and boundedness of the function
x\mapsto(1+x^{2})\exp(-x^{2}/2)
, we find

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.|R_{2}(k,0,\theta)|\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\theta\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(\sqrt{n}K+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\left(\sqrt{n}K+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\theta\sqrt{n}\theta\left(\sqrt{n}K+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\left(K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\left(2K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}|\right)\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

Consequently, by Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{2,1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor ns\rfloor}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{2,2}}
.

By a first order Taylor expansion of
P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)
in the variable
y
around
\rho_{0}
, we get

\displaystyle P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)-P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0};1/n)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=-\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\frac{1}{\beta/n}\left(\frac{\xi_{y}-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2/n}\left(\frac{\xi_{y}-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\right)(y-\rho_{0}),

where for every
y
we denote with
\xi_{y}
the intermediate value appearing in the Lagrange version of the remainder in the Taylor expansion. In particular,
\rho_{0}\leq\xi_{y}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta
. Using boundedness of
x\mapsto x\exp(-x^{2}/4)
then yields

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\left|\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\left(P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)-P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0};1/n)\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}n\theta\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4/n}\left(\frac{\xi_{y}-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}n\theta^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

Then, with Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{2,2}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=3}
.

By (
E.7
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}n\theta\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta}^{\infty}\left(\frac{(y-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt\cdot P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\frac{n\theta^{2}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

Evaluation of the integral gives

\displaystyle n\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta}^{\infty}\left(\frac{(y-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n};1/n)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\frac{2\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(\theta-\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}))^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right),

such that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=-\theta\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\frac{2\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)+r_{n}^{3}(t,\theta),

with
r_{n}^{3}(t,\theta):=r_{n}^{3,1}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{3,2}(t,\theta)
, where

\displaystyle r_{n}^{3,1}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\theta\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\frac{2\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(\theta-\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}))^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\right],

\displaystyle r_{n}^{3,2}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=-\frac{n\theta^{2}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

In what follows, we are going to prove the moment condition on both
r_{n}^{3,1}(t,\theta),r_{n}^{3,2}(t,\theta)
seperately.

-\boldsymbol{r_{n}^{3,1}}
.

Using a first order Taylor expansion of the
\exp
-term, we find with boundedness of
x\mapsto|x|\exp(-x^{2}/4)
that

\displaystyle\left|\exp\left(-\frac{(\theta-\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}))^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\theta\frac{|\xi_{k}-\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})|}{\beta^{2}/n}\exp\left(-\frac{(\xi_{k}-\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}))^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\theta\exp\left(-\frac{(\xi_{k}-\frac{\beta}{\alpha}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}))^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\theta\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}},

for a suitable intermediate value
0\leq\xi_{k}\leq\theta
for every
k=1,\dots,n
. Then, with Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{3,1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

-\boldsymbol{r_{n}^{3,2}}
.

Here, we observe that with Lemma
B.1
and the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\infty}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{-(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{1/n}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

Then, with Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{3,2}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=4}
.

With the expansion (
E.8
) and (
E.9
) we find using
|\theta|\leq 2K/\sqrt{n}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{I_{4,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\theta\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(\sqrt{n}K+n|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(K+\sqrt{n}|y-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(2K+\sqrt{n}|y-\rho_{0}|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(1+|y|)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

As
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
, Lemma
B.1
reveals

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{1}{\theta^{\prime}}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor ns\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{4,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\frac{1}{\theta^{\prime}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{I_{4,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sqrt{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sqrt{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sqrt{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}=C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=5}
.

First, we note that for
\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta
and
\theta\leq K/\sqrt{n}
, Lemma
B.1
reveals

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{\max\{\alpha,\beta\}^{2}}{\min\{\alpha,\beta\}^{2}}\exp\left(\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K).\end{split}

(F.2)

Then, by the expression (
E.11
) and boundedness of
x\mapsto|x|\exp(-x^{2}/4)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{I_{5,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\sqrt{n}\frac{2|y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi+X_{(k-1)/n}|}{\alpha^{2}/n}

\displaystyle\hskip 184.9429pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

As
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
and
\theta\leq K/\sqrt{n}
, Lemma
B.1
reveals

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\left|\frac{1}{\theta^{\prime}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor ns\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{5,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\frac{1}{\theta^{\prime}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{I_{5,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}n\theta^{\prime}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\sqrt{n}\theta\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=6}
.

The same argument as for
j=4
gives with (
E.13
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{1}{\theta^{\prime}}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor ns\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=7}
.

Arguing as for
j=3
, we find with (
E.14
)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{7,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=-\theta\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\frac{2\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)+r_{n}^{7}(t,\theta).

where
r_{n}^{7}(t,\theta):=r_{n}^{7,1}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{7,2}(t,\theta)
with

\displaystyle r_{n}^{7,1}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)^{2}n\theta^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle r_{n}^{7,2}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=-\theta\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\frac{2\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 213.39566pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right).

We discuss these terms seperately:

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{7,1}}
.

Because
\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}
for
\theta^{\prime}\geq 0
, we get along the lines of the evaluation of
r_{n}^{3,2}
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{7,1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{7,2}}
.

Since
\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}=-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
and
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
, by Lemma
B.1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{7,2}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2(k-1)\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=8}
.

By the same arguments used for
j=2
we find with (
E.15
)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{8,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=\theta\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)+r_{n}^{8}(t,\theta),

where
r_{n}^{8}(t,\theta)=r_{n}^{8,1}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{8,2}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{8,3}(t,\theta)
with

\displaystyle r_{n}^{8,1}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[R_{8}(k,0,\theta)\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right],

\displaystyle r_{n}^{8,2}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\left(P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)-P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0};1/n)\right)dy,

\displaystyle r_{n}^{8,3}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\theta\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 213.39566pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right).

We discuss these terms seperately:

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{8,1}}
.

From the bound (
E.16
) for
R_{8}(k,0,\theta)
, Lemma
B.1
and boundedness of the function
x\mapsto(1+x^{2})\exp(-x^{2}/2)
, we find

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.|R_{8}(k,0,\theta)|\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\theta\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(\sqrt{n}K+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left(\sqrt{n}K+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\left(K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\exp\left(\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\theta}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

Now we have, using
X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta
and
0\leq\theta\leq K/\sqrt{n}
that

\displaystyle n(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\theta\leq\begin{cases}8K^{2},&\quad\textrm{ if }X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}\leq 8K/\sqrt{n},\\
\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{8/n},&\quad\textrm{ if }X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}>8K/\sqrt{n}.\end{cases}

(F.3)

In particular

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.|R_{8}(k,0,\theta)|\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{8\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

As the random exp-part of this bound is independent of
\theta
, we easily deduce with Corollary
B.2
that

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{8,1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{8,2}}
.

By a first order Taylor expansion and boundedness of
x\mapsto x\exp(-x^{2}/4)
, we find for intermediate values
\rho_{0}\leq\xi_{y}\leq\rho_{0}+y
appearing in the Lagrange version of the remainder term that

\displaystyle\left|P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)-P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0};1/n)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}(y-\rho_{0})\left[\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\xi_{y}}{\beta^{2}/n}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\xi_{y})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\left.+\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-2\rho_{0}+\xi_{y}}{\beta^{2}/n}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-2\rho_{0}+\xi_{y})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\xi_{y})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-2\rho_{0}+\xi_{y})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left|\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\left(P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},y;1/n)-P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},\rho_{0};1/n)\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\xi_{y})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\left.+\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-2\rho_{0}+\xi_{y})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left(\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\left.+\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{8\beta^{2}/n}\right),

where the second step follows by uniformly bounding the integrand on the integration domain and the last step follows from (
F.3
) as in the treatment of
r_{n}^{8,1}(t,\theta)
. Then, as the random exp-part of this bound is independent of
\theta
, with Corollary
B.2
,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{8,2}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{8,3}}
.

Here, the evaluation is done analogously to that of
r_{n}^{7,2}(t,\theta)
as
r_{n}^{8,3}(t,\theta)
and
r_{n}^{7,2}(t,\theta)
differ only in a factor
C_{\alpha,\beta}
.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=9}
.

By the same arguments used for
j=1
, the remainder
r_{n}^{3,1}(t,\theta)
and the remainder term
r_{n}^{6,2}(t,\theta)
, we find

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{9,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\theta\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)+r_{n}^{9}(t,\theta),

where
r_{n}^{9}(t,\theta):=r_{n}^{9,1}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{9,2}(t,\theta)+r_{n}^{9,3}(t,\theta)
with

\displaystyle r_{n}^{9,1}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\theta\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}+\theta)^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\left.-\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right],

\displaystyle r_{n}^{9,2}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\theta\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right),

\displaystyle r_{n}^{9,3}(t,\theta)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[R_{9}(k,0,\theta)\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

We discuss these terms seperately:

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{9,1}}
.

By a first order Taylor expansion we find with the intermediate value
0\leq\xi_{k}\leq\theta
in the Lagrange remainder,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left|\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}+\theta)^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)-\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\frac{|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k}|}{\beta^{2}/n}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)\sqrt{n}\theta\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{8\beta^{2}/n}\right),

where the second step used boundedness of
x\mapsto|x|\exp(-x^{2}/4)
and the last one follows by (
F.3
) with the same argument used for
r_{n}^{8,1}(t,\theta)
. Thus,

\left|r_{n}^{9,1}(t,\theta)\right|\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

and by Corollary
B.2
,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{9,1}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{9,2}}
.

Here, the evaluation is done analogously to that of
r_{n}^{7,2}(t,\theta)
as
r_{n}^{9,2}(t,\theta)
and
r_{n}^{7,2}(t,\theta)
differ only in a factor
C_{\alpha,\beta}
.

\boldsymbol{-r_{n}^{9,3}}
.

We first observe

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt=\frac{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)}{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\xi_{k})\right)}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\end{split}

(F.4)

for
X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta
. Then, by an analougous computation as in
j=1
for
R_{1}(k,0,\theta)
, we find

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[R_{9}(k,0,\theta)\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}+\theta-\rho_{0}-2\xi)^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n\theta^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right),

where the last step works as with (
F.3
) as in the treatment of
r_{n}^{8,1}(t,\theta)
. Then, by Corollary
B.2
,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}^{9,3}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

Finally, we sum up our results: Defining

r_{n}(t,\theta):=\sum_{j\in\{1,2,3,7,8,9\}}r_{n}^{j}(t,\theta)+\sum_{j\in\{4,5,6\}}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{j,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

we have proven the decomposition

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{9}\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{j,k}^{\theta}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\frac{\theta}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\left(\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\alpha}+\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)-\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\frac{2\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{\theta}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\left(-\frac{2\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\log\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{2}{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{\lfloor nt\rfloor}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+r_{n}(t,\theta).

From this and some simple algebraic manipulations, we get the asserted decomposition in the statement of Proposition
3.1
. Moreover, we have shown that

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta}\sup_{s\leq t}\frac{|r_{n}(s,\theta^{\prime})|}{\theta^{\prime}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)n^{3/2}\theta\sqrt{t}.

The statement for
\theta<0
can be derived in exactly the same way. Here we use that

\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)=-\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)

and then apply the expansions (
E.2
)-(
E.19
) to this right-hand side.
∎

The proof is done via induction on
m
. For the induction hypothesis, we investigate
m=1
and set
d_{1}=d
,
k_{1}=k
and
j_{1}=j
for the ease of notation. We are going to show the claim for
Y_{k}^{j}=Z_{k}^{j}
for each choice of
d\leq D
,
1\leq k\leq n
and
j\in\{1,\dots,9\}
. The claim for
Y_{k}^{j}=\overline{Z}_{k}^{j}
is then a consequence of Jensen’s inequality for conditional expectations. We will repeatedly use that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}\left[(1+|x|)^{d}\exp(-x^{2}/4)\right]&\leq 2^{d}+\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}\left[|x|^{d}\exp(-x^{2}/4)\right]\\
&=2^{d}+(2d)^{d}\exp(-d^{2})\leq 2^{D}+(2D)^{D}=:C(D).\end{split}

(F.5)

Moreover, we will always assume
\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta
and denote by
\xi_{k}
an intermediate value of a corresponding (Taylor-) expansion, in particular, we have
\theta^{\prime}\leq\xi_{k}\leq\theta
.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=1}.

With the expansion (
E.1
) and (
E.3
),

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}\left|\frac{X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n}}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right|^{d}n^{3d/2}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{d(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\\
&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\frac{|X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n}|^{d}n^{d/2}}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\xi_{k}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right).\end{split}

(F.6)

To evaluate the conditional expectation of this bound under
\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}
, we have to distinguish the cases
\theta^{\prime}<0
and
\theta^{\prime}\geq 0
in order to perform the calculation with the correct regime of the transition density.

\boldsymbol{-\theta^{\prime}<0}
.

Here,
\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}\subset\{X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\}
. Then, using (
F.1
), we find from (
F.5
), (
F.6
) and the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}\frac{|y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n}|^{d}}{(1/n)^{d/2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{-\infty}^{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+\theta^{\prime})/\sqrt{\alpha^{2}/n}}|y|^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

\boldsymbol{-\theta^{\prime}\geq 0}
.

Here, we have to work with different regimes of the transition density, depending in which case on the right-hand side of

\displaystyle\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\}\cup\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cup\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cup\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime},\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}

we are in. For the first of them, we get from (
F.1
), (
F.5
), (
F.6
) and the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\}}\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\frac{|y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n}|^{d}}{(1/n)^{d/2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{-\infty}^{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k})/\sqrt{\alpha^{2}/n}}|y|^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

Using (
F.2
) instead of (
F.1
), the same computation reveals

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime},\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

If
X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}
, we have
|X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n}|\leq 3K/\sqrt{n}+|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}|
. After applying (
E.3
) to (
F.6
) another time, we then find with Lemma
B.1
and (
F.5
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\left(3K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}|\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\left(3K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}|\right)^{d}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\left(3K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}|\right)^{d}\theta^{\prime}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

In the remaining case, i.e. for
X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}
, we first apply (
E.3
) to (
F.6
). Then, as
|X_{k/n}-2\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+X_{(k-1)/n}|\leq 3K/\sqrt{n}+|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}|
, we bound with Lemma
B.1
, (
E.3
) and the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\left(3K+\sqrt{n}|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}|\right)^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(3K+\sqrt{n}|y-\rho_{0}|\right)^{d}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(3K+\sqrt{n}|y-\rho_{0}|\right)^{d}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{-\infty}^{\sqrt{n}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})}\left(3K+|y|\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

Now, estimating the expectation of the upper bounds in each of these four cases with Corollary
B.2
gives

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left|Z_{k}^{1}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}

which is the stated bound of the lemma for
j=1
.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=2}.

With the expansion (
E.5
) and (
E.6
) we find with Lemma
B.1
, (
F.5
) and our assumption
|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\leq 2K/\sqrt{n}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Z_{k}^{2}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(2K\sqrt{n}+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)^{d}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\left(2K\sqrt{n}+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}\sqrt{n}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\left(2K\sqrt{n}+n|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}(2K)n^{d/2}\left(2K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\left(4K+\sqrt{n}|X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}|\right)^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

In particular, Corollary
B.2
then implies

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left|Z_{k}^{2}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=3}.

By the expansion (
E.7
), inequality (
F.5
) and the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Z_{k}^{3}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\frac{|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|}{2/\sqrt{n}}+\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\beta/\sqrt{n}}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\alpha/\sqrt{n}}\right)^{d}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 184.9429pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\Big|X_{(k-1)/n}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(K+2\sqrt{n}|X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n}|\right)^{d}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\Big|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}K^{d}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta}^{\infty}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}(D)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta}^{\infty}n^{d/2}|y-X_{(k-1)/n}|^{d}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}(D)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\int_{\sqrt{n}(\rho_{0}+\theta-X_{(k-1)/n})}^{\infty}|y|^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).

Corollary (
B.2
) then reveals

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left|Z_{k}^{3}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=4}.

With the expansion (
E.8
) and (
E.9
) we find using
|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\leq 2K/\sqrt{n}
, (
F.5
) and the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Z_{k}^{4}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(2K\sqrt{n}+n|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)^{d}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}\left(2K+\sqrt{n}|y-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)^{d}

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}\left(4K+\sqrt{n}|y-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime}|\right)^{d}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}(1+|y|)^{d}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

In particular, we find with Lemma
B.1
and
\sqrt{n}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\leq 2K
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left|Z_{k}^{4}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\sqrt{n}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=5}.

Using (
F.2
) and following the steps from
j=1
, by the expression (
E.11
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Z_{k}^{5}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-2\xi_{k}+\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{4/n}\right)

with

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left|Z_{k}^{5}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d}n^{d/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=6}.

This works the same as
j=4
with the bound (
E.13
) on the expansion (
E.12
).

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=7}.

This works the same as
j=3
with the expansion (
E.14
).

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=8}.

This works the same as
j=2
with the expansion (
E.15
) and the bound (
E.16
).

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{j=9}.

This works analogously to
j=1
with the expansion (
E.17
) by replacing the bound (
F.1
) with (
F.4
). Moreover, we now distinguish the cases
\theta\geq 0
and
\theta<0
. In the first one, we have the inclusion
\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta,X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}\subset\{X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}
and we can evaluate the conditional expectation by integrating against
P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}
. For
\theta<0
, we have

\displaystyle\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta,X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0},X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}\}\cup\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0},\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cup\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\}\cup\{\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\},

and use the corresponding regime of the transition density for the first two cases. For the other two, it is sufficient to work with the bound provided in Lemma
B.1
(see
j=1
).

This completes the induction hypothesis for
m=1
. The next step is the induction step
m\mapsto m+1
. Here, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m+1}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Y_{k_{m+1}}^{j_{m+1}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{m+1}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{k_{m+1}-1}\right]\right].

Within the proof of the induction hypothesis
m=1
, we have shown that for all
j_{m+1}\in\{1,\dots,9\}
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left|Z_{k_{m+1}}^{j_{m+1}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{m+1}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{k_{m+1}-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}\Bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\left.+\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k_{m+1}-1}-c(\rho_{0},\theta^{\prime},\theta))^{2}}{c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)/n}\right)\right].

for some constant
c(\rho_{0},\theta^{\prime},\theta)\in\mathbb{R}
and
c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)>0
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m+1}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right]&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right.\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\left.\cdot\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}+\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k_{m+1}-1}-c(\rho_{0},\theta^{\prime},\theta))^{2}}{c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)/n}\right)\right)\right].\end{split}

(F.7)

We now distinguish two cases that both yield the desired claim:

k_{m}=k_{m+1}-1
.

Here, we simply bound

\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}+\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k_{m+1}-1}-c(\rho_{0},\theta^{\prime},\theta))^{2}}{c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)/n}\right)\leq 2

and then the induction hypothesis for
m
reveals from (
F.7
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m+1}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}Cn^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{m}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{i}-k_{i-1}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)n^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m+1}d_{i}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{\sum_{i=1}^{m+1}d_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{m+1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{i}-k_{i-1}}},

where the last step uses that
k_{m+1}-k_{m}=1
.

k_{m}<k_{m+1}-1
.

Here, we use that by Corollary
B.2
,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k_{m+1}-1}-c(\rho_{0},\theta^{\prime},\theta))^{2}}{c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)/n}\right)\right|X_{k_{m}}\right]\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}}{\sqrt{k_{m+1}-k_{m}}}

and by Lemma
B.1
and
|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\leq 2K/\sqrt{n}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{k_{m}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k_{m+1}-k_{m})/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{k_{m}})^{2}}{2(k_{m+1}-k_{m})\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)}{\sqrt{k_{m+1}-k_{m}}}.

Then (
F.7
) reveals together with the induction hypothesis for
m

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m+1}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left.\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k_{m+1}-1}-c(\rho_{0},\theta^{\prime},\theta))^{2}}{c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)/n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{k_{m}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{m+1}-k_{m}}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\prod_{i=1}^{m}\left|Y_{k_{i}}^{j_{i}}(\theta^{\prime},\theta)\right|^{d_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{m+1}-k_{m}}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{d_{m+1}}n^{d_{m+1}/2}Cn^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{m}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{i}-k_{i-1}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(D,K)n^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m+1}d_{i}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|^{\sum_{i=1}^{m+1}d_{i}}\prod_{i=1}^{m+1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{i}-k_{i-1}}}.

∎

G
Remaining proofs of Section
4

This section is divided into two parts: In Subsection
G.1
the remaining proofs for the
\sqrt{n}
-consistency of Subsection
4.1
are given, whereas in Subsection
G.2
those for the proof of the
n
-consistency of Subsection
4.2
may be found.

Recall the definition of
I_{j,k}^{\theta^{\prime},\theta}
given in (
3.2
) and remember that
C_{\alpha,\beta}
denotes some real and positive constant that only depends on
\alpha
and
\beta
but may change from line to line.

G.1
Remaining proofs of Subsection
4.1

The proof makes use of the inequality

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{i}}\ell_{n}(\theta)\leq\sum_{j=1}^{9}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{i}}\mathcal{I}_{j}(\theta)=\sum_{j=1}^{9}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{i}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{j,k}^{\theta}}

for the nine different cases
\mathcal{I}_{1}(\theta),\dots,\mathcal{I}_{9}(\theta)
given in (
3.3
) and investigate all of those seperately. It will turn out that all
\mathcal{I}_{j}(\theta)
for
j\neq 5
are summarized in
F_{n}^{1}(K,L)
,
F_{n}^{2}(K,L)
and the fifth case is split into
N_{n}^{L}(\theta)
and a remainder that also contributes to
F_{n}^{1}(K,L)
and
F_{n}^{2}(K,L)
, respectively.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{1}(\theta)}
: Recall that
I_{1,k}^{\theta}=I_{1,k}
is independ of
\theta
. Using
(1-a)/(1-b)=1+(b-a)/(1-b)
,
\log(1+x)\leq x
for
x>-1
and the fact that for
X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}

1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\geq 1-\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}>0

gives

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\log\left(\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)}{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\left|\exp\left(\frac{2\theta}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})-\frac{2\theta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}/n}\right)-1\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right),

where the last step uses
X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}
. Next, we bound its expectation. First, using that
(a+b)^{2}\geq a^{2}+b^{2}
for
ab\geq 0
, and
