\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}P_{1}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)\leq\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\alpha^{2}/n}}\left(1+\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right),

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(y-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right).

In consequence, by Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}=C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}.

Summarising,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}{p_{1/n}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n})}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{1,k}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)=:\Xi_{n}^{(1)}

with
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(1)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}L
, as
L\geq 1
.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{2}(\theta)}
: With the inequality

\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta}}\left(1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\right)\leq 1+\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}

we deduce

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\beta(\alpha+\beta)}{2\alpha^{2}}\left(1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}+\frac{n}{2}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta}}\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}+n|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}||X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\}}\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right),

where the last step follows as
X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}
. As
x\mapsto(1+x^{2})\exp(-x^{2}/4)
is bounded, we obtain with the Gaussian tail inequality,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\infty}\left(1+n(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt=C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\sqrt{n}(\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)/n})}^{\infty}\left(1+y^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right).\end{split}

(G.1)

With Corollary
B.2
, we then find

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\}}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}

and hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{2,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\leq X_{k/n}\}}\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right):=\Xi_{n}^{(2)}

with
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(2)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}
.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{3}(\theta)}
: By dropping negative terms and using
X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}
and
X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta
on
I_{3,k}^{\theta}
, we have the bound

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{2}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq n(\alpha^{-1}+\beta^{-1})^{2}\left(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{0}},

where the last step uses that the indicator
\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{\theta}}=\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}
is larger, the smaller
\theta
. Hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\log\left(\frac{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq(\alpha^{-1}+\beta^{-1})^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{n}n\left(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{3,k}^{0}}=:\Xi_{n}^{(3)}

By (
G.1
) and Corollary
B.2
we find
\sup_{K\geq 0}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(3)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}
.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{4}(\theta)}
: Follows by the same reasoning as
\mathcal{I}_{2}(\theta)
by interchanging the roles of
\alpha
and
\beta
and gives an upper bound
\Xi_{n}^{(4)}
with
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(4)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}
.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{5}(\theta)}
: First, we split

\displaystyle\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\frac{\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}\right)}{\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)}\frac{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)}{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)}.

Taking the logarithm on both sides then yields

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)=\log\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)-\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2/n}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)+R_{k,\theta}

with

R_{k,\theta}=\log\left(\frac{1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)}{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)}\right).

Denoting

g_{k}:=\log\left(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\right)-\frac{n}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}

(which is independent of
\theta
) and decomposing

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{I_{5,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{5,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left(1-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

then gives the decomposition

\displaystyle\begin{split}\log\left(\frac{P_{1}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{5,k}^{\theta}}&=S_{1,\theta}(k)+S_{2,\theta}(k)+S_{3,\theta}(k)+S_{4,\theta}(k),\end{split}

(G.2)

with

\displaystyle S_{1,\theta}(k)

\displaystyle:=g_{k}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

\displaystyle S_{2,\theta}(k)

\displaystyle:=R_{k,\theta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

\displaystyle S_{3,\theta}(k)

\displaystyle:=-g_{k}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\left(1-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right),

\displaystyle S_{4,\theta}(k)

\displaystyle:=g_{k}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

First, we observe that

\sum_{k=1}^{n}S_{1,\theta}(k)=N_{n}^{L}(\theta).

In what follows, we will construct upper bounds for
\sum_{k=1}^{n}S_{j,\theta}(k)
for
j=2,3,4
and determine upper bounds of their first moments.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{S_{2,\theta}(k)}
. Here, we first observe

\displaystyle S_{2,\theta}(k)

\displaystyle=R_{k,\theta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta,\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\leq|R_{k,\theta}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\}}+|R_{k,\theta}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

For the first term, we use
x/(1+x)\leq\log(1+x)\leq x
for
x\geq-1
and the inequalities

1-\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}\leq 1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\leq 1+\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}

and

1-\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}\leq 1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\leq 1+\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}

to derive

\displaystyle|R_{k,\theta}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}.

For
|R_{k,\theta}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
we simply use that
|R_{k,\theta}|
can be bounded independently of
k
and
\theta
and thus have

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}S_{2,\theta}(k)\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\Xi_{n}^{(5,1)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}\right)

and

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}S_{2,\theta}(k)\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}+\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}\right),

where

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,1)}

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)}

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}},

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

In what follows, we will derive deterministic bounds on sets with high probability for
\Xi_{n}^{(5,1)}
,
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)},\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)},\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}
and an
L^{1}
-bound on
\Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}
. To this aim, we consider

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle=\{\overline{X}>\rho_{0}+\gamma,\underline{X}<\rho_{0}-\gamma\},

\displaystyle A_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\left\{\inf_{y\in[\rho_{0}-\gamma/2,\rho_{0}+\gamma/2]}L_{1}^{y}(X)>\xi_{l}\right\}\cap\left\{\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}}L_{1}^{y}(X)\leq\xi_{u}\right\},

\displaystyle A_{4}(n)

\displaystyle=\left\{\sup_{|t-s|<1/n}|X_{t}-X_{s}|\leq n^{-4/9}\right\}.

The set
A_{2}
was given in (
4.2
),
A_{4}(n)
was given in (
4.4
). For those, we have chosen the parameter
\gamma
such that
\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}(A_{i}|L_{1}^{\rho_{0}}(X)>0)\geq 1-\epsilon
,
i=2,4
for
n
sufficiently large. To choose
\xi_{l},\xi_{u}
to ensure
\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}(A_{3}^{\prime})\geq 1-\epsilon
, we first choose
\xi_{l}
sufficiently small such that the first set in the definition of
A_{3}^{\prime}
has probability
\geq 1-\epsilon/2
. This is possible, as this set coincides with
A_{3}
given in (
4.3
). Now choose a compact interval
[-a,a]
such that for
A=\{X_{s}\notin[-a,a]\textrm{ for some }s\in[0,1]\}
, we have
\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}(A)<\epsilon/4
which is possible by the argument used to bound the probability of
A_{1}
in (
4.1
). By Corollary
29.18
in
Kallenberg (2021)
,

\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}}L_{1}^{y}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{A^{c}}=\sup_{y\in[-a,a]}L_{1}^{y}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{A^{c}}.

Moreover, as
L_{1}^{\bullet}(X)
is continuous by Corollary
1.8
on p.
226
in
Revuz and Yor (1999)
, and attains its maximum and minimum on the compact set
[-a,a]
,
\sup_{y\in[-a,a]}L_{1}^{y}(X)
is a
\mathbb{R}
-valued random variable and consequently, it is tight. Thus,
\xi_{u}
can be chosen large enough such that

\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}\left(\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}}L_{1}^{y}>\xi_{u}\right)\leq\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}\left(A\right)+\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}\left(\sup_{y\in[-a,a]}L_{1}^{y}>\xi_{u}\right)\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}.

\boldsymbol{-\ \Xi_{n}^{(5,1)}}
.

Defining

G_{n,\theta}(x):=\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(x-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

we have by Lemma
G.2
that the probability of the event

\displaystyle E_{5,1}:=\left\{\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}<\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}G_{n,\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}(X_{s})ds\right|\leq\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}(X_{s})ds\right\}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cap A_{2}\cap A_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\supset\left\{\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}<\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}G_{n,\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}(X_{s})ds\right|\leq\frac{\alpha^{2}\xi_{l}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right\}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cap A_{2}\cap A_{3}^{\prime}

is larger than
1-3\epsilon
for
n
sufficiently large. Here, we used that on
A_{3}^{\prime}
we have
L_{1}^{y}(X)>\xi_{l}
for
\rho_{0}-\gamma/2\leq y\leq\rho_{0}+\gamma/2
and consequently by the occupation times formula

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}(X_{s})\sigma(X_{s})^{2}ds

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}G_{n,\theta}(y)L_{1}^{y}(X)dy

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\exp\left(\frac{2y}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}\right)L_{1}^{y+\rho_{0}+\theta}dy

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\sqrt{n}\xi_{l}}{\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{-\gamma/2}^{0}\exp\left(\frac{2y}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}\right)dy

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha^{2}\xi_{l}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\left(1-\exp\left(-\frac{\gamma}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}\right)\right)\geq\frac{\alpha^{2}\xi_{l}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}},

where the last step is true for
n
large enough to ensure
\exp(-\gamma\sqrt{n}/\alpha^{2})\leq 1/2
. Then, on
E_{5,1}
we have the bound

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,1)}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}2n\int_{0}^{1}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{s}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{s}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}ds

\displaystyle\leq 2n\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\frac{\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(y-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha^{2}\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\sqrt{n}.

\boldsymbol{-\ \tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)}}
.

On
A_{2}\cap A_{4}(n)
, we find with the occupation times formula the upper bound

\displaystyle\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta-n^{-4/9}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-n^{-4/9}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(-\frac{2n^{1/18}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)+\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}n\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\rho_{0}+\theta-2n^{-4/9},\rho_{0}+\theta+n^{-4/9}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\leq n\exp\left(-\frac{2n^{1/18}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)+\frac{3}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\xi_{u}n^{5/9}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(\xi_{u})n^{5/9}.

\boldsymbol{-\ \Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}}
.

As in the treatment of
\mathcal{I}_{1}(\theta)
, one can easily see that
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}
.

\boldsymbol{-\ \Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}}
.

We define

G_{n,\theta}^{\prime}(x):=\mathbbm{1}_{[\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n},\rho_{0}+\theta]}(x)

and the event

\displaystyle E_{5,2}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}<\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}G_{n,\theta}^{\prime}(X_{k/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}^{\prime}(X_{s})ds\right|\leq\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}^{\prime}(X_{s})ds\right\}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cap A_{2}\cap A_{3}^{\prime}.

Similar to
E_{5,1}
we find
\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}>1-\epsilon
for
n
large enough and the bound

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{5,2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}2n\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n},\rho_{0}+\theta]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2n\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}1dy=\frac{2\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\sqrt{n}.

\boldsymbol{-\ \tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}}
.

Here, we use that
X_{k/n}\in[\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n},\rho_{0}+\theta]
implies on the event
A_{4}(n)
that
X_{s}\in[\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}-n^{-4/9},\rho_{0}+\theta+n^{-4/9}]
for all
(k-1)/n\leq s\leq k/n
. In particular,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}^{\prime}\cap A_{4}(n)}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{A_{3}^{\prime}}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}n\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}-n^{-4/9},\rho_{0}+\theta+n^{-4/9}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}-n^{-4/9}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta+n^{-4/9}}1dy\leq\frac{3\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}n^{5/9}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{S_{3,\theta}(k)}
. Here, we have the bound

S_{3,\theta}(k)\leq|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\leq\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}+|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}.

The first summand is already independent of
\theta
and we have in particular

\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}S_{3,\theta}(k)\leq\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,5)}\quad\textrm{ and }\quad\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}S_{3,\theta}(k)\leq\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,5)},

where

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}

\displaystyle\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,5)}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\leq\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}.

In what follows, we will derive deterministic bounds on sets with high probability for
\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}
,
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}
and an
L^{1}
-bound on
\Xi_{n}^{(5,5)}
.

-\ \boldsymbol{\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}}
.

We first note that

\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}.

Then we decompose

\displaystyle\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\left.-\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right).

By Lemma
G.3
, the
L^{1}(\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}})
-norm of the second summand is bounded by
C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/8}\log(n)
. For the first one, we begin estimating with Lemma
B.1
and using boundedness of
x\mapsto x^{2}\exp(-x^{2}/4)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta}^{\infty}\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\sqrt{n}(\rho_{0}+\theta-X_{(k-1)/n})}^{\infty}\left(1+\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\}}\exp\left(\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/\sqrt{n}}\right).

The expression
\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
can be bounded analogously to
\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}
. For the other term we define

\displaystyle G_{n,\theta}^{\prime\prime}(x)

\displaystyle:=\exp\left(\frac{(x-\rho_{0}-\theta)}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/\sqrt{n}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}},

and the event

\displaystyle E_{5,3}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}<\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}G_{n,\theta}^{\prime\prime}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}^{\prime\prime}(X_{s})ds\right|\leq\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}G_{n,\theta}^{\prime\prime}(X_{s})ds\right\}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cap A_{2}\cap A_{3}^{\prime}

Similar to
E_{5,1}
, we find
\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}(E_{5,3})>1-\epsilon
for
n
large enough.
On these two set, we then obtain the bound

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\}}\exp\left(\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/\sqrt{n}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{E_{5,3}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}C_{\alpha,\beta}2n\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{s}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(\frac{(X_{s}-\rho_{0}-\theta)}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/\sqrt{n}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\xi_{u}n}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\sup_{\theta\in\Theta_{1}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\exp\left(\frac{(y-\rho_{0}-\theta)}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/\sqrt{n}}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\xi_{u}\sqrt{n}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\exp\left(\frac{y}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy.

\boldsymbol{-\ \tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}}
.

On
A_{4}(n)
we have

|g_{k}|\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\left(1+nn^{-8/9}\right)\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{1/9}.

Moreover, again on
A_{3}^{\prime}\cap A_{4}(n)
we find with the occupation times formula

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-n^{-4/9}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta+n^{-4/9}\}}

\displaystyle\leq n\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[\rho_{0}+\theta-2n^{-4/9},\rho_{0}+\theta+2n^{-4/9}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\leq n\frac{4\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}n^{-4/9}=\frac{4\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}n^{5/9}.

Both bounds do not depend on
\theta
such that on
A_{3}^{\prime}\cap A_{4}(n)
we finally have the bound

\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}\leq\frac{4C_{\alpha,\beta}\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}n^{2/3}.

\boldsymbol{-\ \Xi_{n}^{(5,5)}}
.

By Lemma
B.1
, Corollary
B.2
and boundedness of
x\mapsto x\exp(-x^{2}/4)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\leq\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(1+n(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\left.\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{-\sqrt{n}(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})}\left(1+y^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}}{\sqrt{k-1}}.

In particular, we have
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(5,5)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}
as desired.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{S_{4,\theta}(k)}
. This term is straigtforward. We estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}g_{k}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}g_{k}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}=:\Xi_{n}^{(5,6)},

which is independent of
\theta
. By Lemma
B.1
and Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|g_{k}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(1+n(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\left.\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(1+y^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}L}{\sqrt{k}}

and thus
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(5,6)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}L
.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{6}(\theta)}
: With the inequality

\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta}}\left(1-\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\right)\geq 1-\frac{|\alpha-\beta|}{\alpha+\beta}

we deduce

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{P_{3}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{2\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}\left(1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)\right)^{-1}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(\frac{(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}/n}-\frac{n}{2}\left(\frac{X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\beta}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{6,k}^{\theta}}\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)^{2}+n|X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta||X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta|\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right),

where the last step uses that on
I_{6,k}^{\theta}
we have
X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}
. The claim follows now along the lines of the treatment of the remainder terms
\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}
,
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}
and gives upper bounds
\Xi_{n}^{(6)}
and
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(6)}
on a set with high probability, where
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(6)}|]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}
and
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(6)}|]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{2/3}
, respectively.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{7}(\theta)}
: This follows by the same reasoning as
\mathcal{I}_{3}(\theta)
and provides an upper bound
\Xi_{n}^{(7)}
with
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(7)}|/\sqrt{n}]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{8}(\theta)}
: Along the lines of
\mathcal{I}_{2}(\theta)
, we find

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{P_{4}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{I_{8,k}^{\theta}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\left(1+n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right).

Then the claim follows along the lines of the treatment of the remainder terms
\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}
,
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}
by replacing
\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta<X_{k/n}\}}
with
\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}
. On a set with high probability, this yields upper bounds
\Xi_{n}^{(8)}
and
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(8)}
with
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(8)}|]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}
and
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(8)}|]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{2/3}
, respectively.

\boldsymbol{\mathcal{I}_{9}(\theta)}
: Similar as for
I_{1}(\theta)
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{I_{9,k}^{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\mathbbm{1}_{I_{9,k}^{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)}{1+\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0})(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{9,k}^{\theta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\left|\exp\left(-\frac{2\theta}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)-1\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{9,k}^{\theta}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right),

where the last step uses
X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta,X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta
. From this, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{I_{9,k}^{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}{P_{2}^{\rho_{0}}(X_{(k-1)/n},X_{k/n};1/n)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta+1/\sqrt{n}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta+1/\sqrt{n}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/n}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta+1/\sqrt{n}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}>\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\exp\left(-\frac{2}{\beta^{2}/\sqrt{n}}(X_{k/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta)\right).

The first summand can now be treated as the same part appearing in
\Xi_{n}^{(5,4)}
in
\mathcal{I}_{5}(\theta)
with the slightly different indicator
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta-1/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}
and the second one as
\Xi_{n}^{(5,1)}
,
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)}
in the discussion of
\mathcal{I}_{5}(\theta)
. In particular, on a set with high probability, we get upper bounds
\Xi_{n}^{(9)}
and
\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(9)}
with
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\Xi_{n}^{(9)}|]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}
and
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(9)}|]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{2/3}
, respectively.

To sum up, we have shown the statement of the lemma with

F_{n}^{1}(K,L):=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1,\dots,9\\
j\neq 5\end{subarray}}\Xi_{n}^{(j)}+\sum_{l=1}^{6}\Xi_{n}^{(5,l)}

and

F_{n}^{2}(K,L):=\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,1)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,2)}+\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,3)}+\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(5,4)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,5)}+\Xi_{n}^{(5,6)}+\sum_{j=1}^{4}\Xi_{n}^{(j)}+\sum_{j=6}^{9}\tilde{\Xi}_{n}^{(j)}.

∎

In this proof, we set
B_{k}:=\{\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}
. Decomposing
1=\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}
, reveals by the triangle inequality

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]-\beta^{2}\right|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt+\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|.\end{split}

(G.3)

Subsequently, both summmands will be bounded seperately. The first expectation on the right-hand side of (
G.3
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{-\infty}^{\rho_{0}}\left(n(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi/n}}\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta}\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\exp\left(-\frac{n}{2}\left(\frac{y-\rho_{0}}{\alpha}-\frac{X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}}{\beta}\right)^{2}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84544pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\frac{2\alpha^{2}\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{-(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}\left(\left(y-\frac{(\beta-\alpha)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})}{\alpha\beta/\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}-\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy.

Now, using
X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}\geq L/\sqrt{n}
on
B_{k}
,
x\exp(-x/4)\leq 4e^{-1}
for
x\geq 0
and the Gaussian tail inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{-\infty}^{-(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}\left(\left(y-\frac{(\beta-\alpha)(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})}{\alpha\beta/\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}-\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\int_{-\infty}^{-L/\beta}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}n(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{-(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{-L/\beta}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy+C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}n(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{-L/\beta}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy+C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{-L/\beta}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy+C_{\alpha,\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{L^{2}}{4\beta^{2}}\right).

Consequently, for every
\epsilon>0
there exists
L=L(\epsilon)>0
large enough such that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}<\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}.

(G.4)

For the second expectation on the right-hand side of (
G.3
), we find

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\infty}\left(n(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{\rho_{0}}^{\infty}\left(n(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\beta^{2}/n}}\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-2\rho_{0}+X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\beta^{2}\left|\int_{(\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)/n})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\beta^{2}\left|\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\alpha+\beta}\int_{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\left(\left(y-\frac{2(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})}{\beta/\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}-1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\right|.

Note that we have

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\beta^{2}\left|\int_{(\rho_{0}-X_{(k-1)/n})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\leq\beta^{2}\left|\int_{-L/\beta}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}-1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\right|

and we see that
L
can be chosen large enough such that the right-hand side is bounded from above by
\epsilon/4
, because
\int_{\mathbb{R}}(y^{2}-1)\exp(-y^{2}/2)dy=0
. Moreover, using again the inequality
x\exp(-x/4)\leq 4e^{-1}
for
x\geq 0
and the Gaussian tail inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\left|\int_{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\left(\left(y-\frac{2(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})}{\beta/\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}-1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 2\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\int_{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt+\frac{8(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{\beta^{2}/n}\int_{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})/\sqrt{\beta^{2}/n}}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 2\int_{L/\beta}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\frac{4(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{\beta^{2}/n}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{2\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 2\int_{L/\beta}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy+\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\frac{4}{e}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0})^{2}}{4\beta^{2}/n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 2\int_{L/\beta}^{\infty}\left(y^{2}+1\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy+\frac{4}{e}\exp\left(-\frac{L^{2}}{4\beta^{2}}\right).

Hence, we can choose
L=L(\epsilon)
large enough such that this bound is again bounded by
\epsilon/4
. In particular, we have shown that for every
\epsilon>0
there exists an
L=L(\epsilon)>0
large enough such that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(n(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\beta^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{k}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}\}}|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}

and the claim follows by combining this with (
G.3
) and (
G.4
).
∎

Exploiting the martingale structure of
N_{n}-\overline{N}_{n}
, one can easily see that (details are given direcly after this proof)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-N_{n}^{L}(\theta^{\prime})+\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta^{\prime})-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)^{2}\right]\leq cn|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|

(G.5)

for some constant
c=c(\alpha,\beta)>0
. With
\psi(x)=x^{2}
and the metric
\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime}):=\sqrt{cn|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|}
, we obtain

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\psi\left(\frac{N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-N_{n}^{L}(\theta^{\prime})+\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta^{\prime})-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)}{\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})}\right)\right]\leq 1.

As the piecewise constant function
\theta\mapsto N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)
is càdlàg, we have

\sup_{\theta\in S_{n,j}}\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)=\sup_{\theta\in S_{n,j}\cap\mathbb{Q}}\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)

and thus can work on a countable set. For an enumeration
(\theta_{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}}
of
[0,2^{j+1}/\sqrt{n}]\cap\mathbb{Q}\supset S_{n,j}\cap\mathbb{Q}
, by monotone convergence,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in[0,2^{j+1}/\sqrt{n}]\cap\mathbb{Q}}\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)\right]=\lim_{m\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in\{\theta_{1},\dots,\theta_{m}\}}\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)\right].

Hence, it is enough to evaluate the supremum over any finite subset
\mathcal{T}^{n}
of
[0,2^{j+1}/\sqrt{n}]\cap\mathbb{Q}
. Without loss of generality, we assume
\theta\in\mathcal{T}^{n}
and set
0=\theta_{0}^{n}\in\mathcal{T}^{n}
. Furthermore, we define

\delta_{0}^{n}:=\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}^{n}}\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta_{0}^{n})\leq\sqrt{cn2^{j+1}/\sqrt{n}}=\sqrt{C_{2}}2^{(j+1)/2}n^{1/4}

and
\delta_{k}^{n}:=2^{-k}\delta_{0}^{n}
for
k\in\mathbb{N}
. Now we inductively define maximal subsets
\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}\subset\mathcal{T}^{n}
such that

\mathcal{T}_{k-1}^{n}\subset\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}\qquad\textrm{ and }\qquad\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\leq\delta_{k}^{n}\textrm{ for different }\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}.

In particular, the cardinality
\#\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}
is bounded by

\#\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}\leq D\left(\delta_{k}^{n},\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n}\right)=\max\{\#\mathcal{T}_{0}:\mathcal{T}_{0}\subset\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\geq\delta_{k}^{n}\textrm{ for different }\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{0}\}

for all
k\in\mathbb{N}
and we have

\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}}\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\leq\delta_{k}^{n}\quad\textrm{ for all }\theta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}^{n}\textrm{ and }k\in\mathbb{N}.

By construction, it is now possible to find for each
k\in\mathbb{N}
and
\theta\in\mathcal{T}^{n}
a point
\pi_{k}^{n}\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}
such that
\rho_{n}(\theta,\pi_{k}^{n}\theta)\leq\delta_{k}^{n}
and for
Z_{n}(\theta)=N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)
we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k+1}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)|&\leq\max_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k+1}^{n}}\left(|Z_{n}(\pi_{k}^{n}\theta)|+|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\pi_{k}^{n}\theta)|\right)\\
&\leq\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)|+\max_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k+1}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\pi_{k}^{n}\theta)|.\end{split}

(G.6)

Using Pisier’s inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\max_{j=1,\dots,m}|Y_{i}|\right]\leq\phi^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[\phi(Y_{i})]\right)

(G.7)

for arbitrary real-valued random variables
Y_{1},\dots,Y_{m}
and even, convex
\phi
with
\phi(0)=0
,
\phi\neq 0
and
\phi^{-1}(v):=\max\{u\geq 0:\phi(u)\leq v\}
, we find with
\phi=\psi
in (
G.7
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\max_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k+1}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\pi_{k}^{n}\theta)|\right]&=\delta_{k}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\max_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k+1}^{n}}\frac{|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\pi_{k}^{n}\theta)|}{\delta_{k}}\right]\\
&\leq\delta_{k}\psi^{-1}\left(D(\delta_{k+1}^{n},\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n})\right)\\
&=4\left(\delta_{k+1}^{n}-\delta_{k+2}^{n}\right)\psi^{-1}\left(D(\delta_{k+1}^{n},\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n})\right)\\
&\leq 4\int_{\delta_{k+2}^{n}}^{\delta_{k+1}^{n}}\psi^{-1}\left(D(u,\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n})\right)du.\end{split}

(G.8)

Combining this inequality for all
k=1,\dots,k_{0}
, where
k_{0}
is the largest value for
k
such that
\delta_{k-1}^{n}\geq 1
and using our particular choice
\psi(x)=x^{2}
from above as well as
Z_{n}(0)=N_{n}^{L}(0)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(0)=0
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}^{n}}\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)\right]\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 4\int_{1}^{\delta_{0}^{n}/2}\sqrt{D(u,\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n})}du+\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}+1}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\pi_{k_{0}}^{n}\theta)|\right].\end{split}

(G.9)

To evaluate the first integral, we note that

D\left(u,\mathcal{T}^{n},\rho_{n}\right)\leq D\left(u,[0,2^{j+1}/\sqrt{n},\rho_{n}\right)\leq c2^{j+1}\sqrt{n}u^{-2}.

Next, we treat the remainder in (
G.9
). For
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}}^{n}
we have
\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\leq 1
by definition of
\mathcal{T}_{k}^{n}
and
k_{0}
. In particular we have
|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\leq(cn)^{-1}
. Denoting by
U_{\epsilon}(x)
an
\epsilon
-environment of
x
, we thus have by using (
G.7
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}+1}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\pi_{k_{0}}^{n}\theta)|\right]&\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}}^{n}}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\theta^{\prime})|\right]\\
&\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}}^{n}}\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\in U_{1/(cn)}(\theta)}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\theta^{\prime})|\right]\\
&\leq\left(\sum_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}}^{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\in U_{1/(cn)}(\theta)}|Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\theta^{\prime})|^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

(G.10)

First, note that the number of elements contained in
\mathcal{T}_{k_{0}}^{n}
is bounded from above by
4c\sqrt{n}2^{j+1}
since
\delta_{k_{0}}^{n}\geq 1/2
. Second, we can bound (assuming wlog
\theta<\theta^{\prime}
)

\displaystyle Z_{n}(\theta)-Z_{n}(\theta^{\prime})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=n\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left((X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}]\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\geq\rho_{0}+L/\sqrt{n}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq n\left|\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left((X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}]\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta+1/(cn)\}},

which is independent of
\theta^{\prime}
. This is due to the special structure of the martingale part
N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)
and the essential observation that makes the bound (
G.9
) useful. Using the estimate
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
and Lemma
B.3
, we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(n\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left((X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}]\right)\right.\right.\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\cdot\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta+1/(cn)\}}\Big)^{2}\right]\leq c^{\prime},\end{split}

where the constant
c^{\prime}=c^{\prime}(\alpha,\beta)>0
is independent of
n
and
\theta
. By using this bound, we get from (
G.9
) and (
G.10
) that for some constant
C_{2}=C_{2}(\alpha,\beta)>0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta\in\mathcal{T}^{n}}\left(N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 4\int_{1}^{\sqrt{c}2^{j/2}n^{1/4}}\sqrt{c}2^{(j+1)/2}n^{1/4}u^{-1}du+2\sqrt{cc^{\prime}}2^{(j+1)/2}n^{1/4}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=C_{2}2^{(j+1)/2}n^{1/4}\left(j\log(2)+\log(n)\right).

∎

Note that
N_{n}^{L}(\theta)-N_{n}^{L}(\theta^{\prime})+\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta^{\prime})-\overline{N}_{n}^{L}(\theta)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})
, where
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\mid\mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}]=0
. Hence, for
l<k
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})d_{l}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})d_{l}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\mid\mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{l}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\mid\mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right]=0

and consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})^{2}\right].

(G.11)

For
\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta
,

\displaystyle d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})

\displaystyle=n\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)\left((X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}]\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}.

Using that by Lemma
B.1

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.n^{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{4}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}n^{2}(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{4}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt=C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}y^{4}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy\leq C_{\alpha,\beta},\end{split}

(G.12)

and
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
yields again with Lemma
B.1

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 2\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\Big[\left(n^{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{4}+n^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 4\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[n^{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{4}\mid X_{(k-1)/n}\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq 4C_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi(k-1)/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k/n}}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|.

We then arrive at

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[d_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\sum_{k=2}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|

and (
G.5
) follows.
∎

The following result may be well-known, yet we did not find an appropriate reference in the literature.

Lemma G.1
.

Let
f_{n}:\mathbb{R}\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}
,
n\in\mathbb{N}
, be measurable functions such that for
m=1,2
,
\int_{\mathbb{R}}|x|^{m}|f_{n}(x)|dx<\infty
,
\|f_{n}\|_{L^{1}}\leq\kappa/\sqrt{n}
,
\|f_{n}\|_{\textrm{sup}}\leq\kappa
and
\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|]\leq\kappa/\sqrt{k}
for all
1\leq k\leq n+1
and some constant
\kappa>0
. Then we have

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right)^{2}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(\kappa)n^{-1/2}.

The proof makes use of the following inequality several times: For every function
f_{n}
that satisfies the assumptions of the lemma, there exists a constant
C_{\alpha,\beta}
that is independent of
f_{n}
such that for all
0<s<t

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[f_{n}(X_{u+t})-f_{n}(X_{u+s})\mid X_{u}\right]\right|\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{t-s}{s^{3/2}}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{1}}.

(G.13)

This inequality follows direcly from item (v) in Lemma
3
in
Mazzonetto (2026)
. To start with the proof of the claim, we first decompose

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right)^{2}\right]\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt=\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right]\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right.\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\left.-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right].\end{split}

(G.14)

For
k=l,l-1
we directly get the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\left|f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-n\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{2\kappa}{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}}{n\sqrt{k}}.

For
k<l-1
we get

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\ \right|\mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\left(f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-f_{n}(X_{s})\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\Big|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-f_{n}(X_{s})\mid\mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}\right]\Big|ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq_proof:inequality_semigroup}}}{{\leq}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{(s-(k-1)/n)-(l-k)/n}{((l-k)/n)^{3/2}}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{1}}ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\int_{0}^{1/n}\frac{s}{((l-k)/n)^{3/2}}ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa}{(l-k)^{3/2}}n\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\int_{0}^{1/n}sds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa}{(l-k)^{3/2}}\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}}{(l-k)^{3/2}}\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

We now find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})\right]\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq 3C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}+2C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n-2}\sum_{l=k+2}^{n}\frac{1}{(l-k)^{3/2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\\
&\leq 3C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}n^{-1/2}+2C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}\frac{1}{n}\int_{1}^{n-2}\int_{k+2}^{n}\frac{1}{(l-k)^{3/2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}dldk\\
&=3C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}n^{-1/2}+2C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}\frac{1}{n}\int_{1}^{n-2}\left(\sqrt{2}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{n-k}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}dk\\
&\leq 3C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}n^{-1/2}+4C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}\frac{1}{n}\int_{1}^{n-2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}dk\\
&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}n^{-1/2}.\end{split}

(G.15)

For the second term in our initial decomposition (
G.14
), we apply a similar argument. For this, we first deduce from (
G.13
) that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|-\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sqrt{n}\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\Big|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|]-\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|f_{n}(X_{s})|]\Big|ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sqrt{n}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{1}}\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\frac{s-(k-1)/n}{((k-1)/n)^{3/2}}ds\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k^{-3/2}.

From this, we consequently derive

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\right]&\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[|f_{n}(X_{(k-1)/n})|]+C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k^{-3/2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\kappa k^{-1/2}+C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa k^{-3/2}\right)\\
&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa\frac{1}{\sqrt{nk}}.\end{split}

(G.16)

Let
k=l,l-1
. Then we find with (
G.16
)

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\left|n\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}f_{n}(X_{s})-f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{2\kappa}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}}{n\sqrt{k}}.

Moreover, for
k<l-1
we can derive with (
G.13
) and (
G.16
)

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\right|\mathcal{F}_{k/n}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}f_{n}(X_{s})-f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{k/n}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\left|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[f_{n}(X_{s})-f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\mid\mathcal{F}_{k/n}\right]\right|ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{1}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\frac{s-k/n-(l-1-k)/n}{((l-k-1)/n)^{3/2}}ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa n^{3/2}\frac{1}{(l-k-1)^{3/2}}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}|f_{n}(X_{s})|ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{(l-k-1)^{3/2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

Then, we conclude analogously as in (
G.15
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\int_{(l-1)/n}^{l/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}f_{n}(X_{(l-1)/n})\int_{(k-1)/n}^{k/n}\sqrt{n}f_{n}(X_{s})ds\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\kappa^{2}n^{-1/2}

as desired.
∎

Lemma G.2
.

Let
K,\xi_{u}>0
and
E:=\{L_{1}^{y}(X)\leq\xi_{u}\textrm{ for all }y\in\mathbb{R}\}
. Then we have

\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}\leq\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}f_{n,\theta}^{(i)}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}f_{n,\theta}^{(i)}(X_{s})ds\right|\mathbbm{1}_{E}\longrightarrow_{\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}}0

for

\displaystyle f_{n,\theta}^{(1)}(x)

\displaystyle:=\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(x),

\displaystyle f_{n,\theta}^{(2)}(x)

\displaystyle:=\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta,a+\theta+L/\sqrt{n}]}(x),

\displaystyle f_{n,\theta}^{(3)}(x)

\displaystyle:=\mathbbm{1}_{A_{\theta}}(x)\exp(-\sqrt{n}C|x-a-\theta|),

where
a\in\mathbb{R}
,
C,L>0
and
A_{\theta}=(-\infty,a+\theta]
or
A_{\theta}=[a+\theta,\infty)
.

The proof is built on a so-called bracketing argument. We start with
f_{n,\theta}^{(1)}
. Let
\epsilon>0
and

\displaystyle\theta_{j}:=K/\sqrt{n}+j\epsilon\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(2\xi_{u})^{-1}/\sqrt{n}

(G.17)

for
j=0,1,\dots,\lceil 2\xi_{u}(\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}\epsilon)^{-1}(n^{1/4}-K)\rceil
. In particular, for every
K/\sqrt{n}<\theta<n^{-1/4}
there exists
j_{0}
such that
\theta_{j_{0}}\leq\theta\leq\theta_{j_{0}+1}
and

\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}}]}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}.

From this, we find on
E

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{s})-\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{s})ds+\epsilon,

where we use that by the occupation times formula

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{E}\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}]}(X_{s})-\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}}]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\mathbbm{1}_{E}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\left(\int_{a+\theta_{j_{0}}-L/\sqrt{n}}^{a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n}}L_{1}^{y}(X)dy+\int_{a+\theta_{j_{0}}}^{a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}}L_{1}^{y}(X)dy\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{2\sqrt{n}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\xi_{u}\left(\theta_{j_{0}+1}-\theta_{j_{0}}\right)=\epsilon.

Similarly, we derive on
E

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{s})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j_{0}+1}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j_{0}}]}(X_{s})ds-\epsilon.

Combining these bounds and abbreviating
c:=2\xi_{u}\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}^{-1}
then yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}\leq\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{s})ds\right|\mathbbm{1}_{E}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\leq\max_{j=1,\dots,\lceil c\epsilon^{-1}n^{1/4}\rceil}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{s})ds\right|+\epsilon.\end{split}

(G.18)

In the next step, we want to apply Lemma
G.1
to
f_{n,\theta}^{(1)}
. This is possible, because this function is clearly bounded by
1
,
\int_{\mathbb{R}}f_{n}^{(1)}(x)|x|^{m}dx<\infty
for
m=1,2
,

\left\|f_{n,\theta}^{(1)}\right\|_{L^{1}}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(x)dx=\frac{L}{\sqrt{n}},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{(k-1)/n})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n}}^{a+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{L}{\sqrt{k-1}}

by Lemma
B.1
, where all bounds are independent of
\theta
. Then, we have with Lemma
G.1

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}\left(\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}\leq\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta]}(X_{s})ds\right|>2\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}_{\rho_{0}}\left(\max_{j=1,\dots,\lceil c\epsilon^{-1}n^{1/4}\rceil}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{s})ds\right|>\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{\lceil c\epsilon^{-1}n^{1/4}\rceil}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{[a+\theta_{j}-L/\sqrt{n},a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{s})ds\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon^{2}}C_{\alpha,\beta}(L)n^{-1/2}n^{1/4}\longrightarrow 0

which completes the proof for the function
f_{n,\theta}^{(1)}
. The function
f_{n,\theta}^{(2)}
works exaktly the same way. For
f_{n,\theta}^{(3)}
with
A_{\theta}=(-\infty,a+\theta]
, we first recall
\theta_{j}
from (
G.17
) and define the brackets

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j}]}(x)\exp(C\sqrt{n}(x-a-\theta_{j+1}))

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta]}(x)\exp(C\sqrt{n}(x-a-\theta))

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j+1}]}(x)\exp(C\sqrt{n}(x-a-\theta_{j})).

for
\theta_{j}\leq\theta\leq\theta_{j+1}
. For these upper and lower bounds we find with the occupation times formula on
E

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j+1}]}(X_{s})\exp(C\sqrt{n}(X_{s}-a-\theta_{j}))\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\left.-\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{s})\exp(C\sqrt{n}(X_{s}-a-\theta_{j+1}))\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j+1}]}(y)\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j}))\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\left.-\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j}]}(y)\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j+1}))\right)L_{1}^{y}(X)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{-\infty}^{a+\theta_{j}}\left(\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j}))-\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j+1}))\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\frac{\sqrt{n}\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\int_{a+\theta_{j}}^{a+\theta_{j+1}}\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j}))dy.

By a Taylor expansion with intermediate point
\theta_{j}\leq\zeta\leq\theta_{j+1}
(possibly depending on
C,n,j
) we find

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{a+\theta_{j}}\left(\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j}))-\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j+1}))\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\left(\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}\right)\int_{-\infty}^{a+\theta_{j}}C\sqrt{n}\exp\left(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\zeta)\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\left(\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}\right)\int_{-\infty}^{C\sqrt{n}(\theta_{j}-\zeta)}e^{y}dy\leq\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}

and

\displaystyle\int_{a+\theta_{j}}^{a+\theta_{j+1}}\exp(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta_{j}))dy

\displaystyle\leq\left(\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}\right)\exp\left(C\sqrt{n}\left(\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}\right)\exp\left(\frac{C\epsilon\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}{2\xi_{u}}\right),

which then yields on
E
the bound

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j+1}]}(X_{s})\exp(C\sqrt{n}(X_{s}-a-\theta_{j}))\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\left.-\mathbbm{1}_{(-\infty,a+\theta_{j}]}(X_{s})\exp(C\sqrt{n}(X_{s}-a-\theta_{j+1}))\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}\xi_{u}}{\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\left(\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}\right)\left(1+\exp\left(\frac{C\epsilon\min\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}{2\xi_{u}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\tilde{C}\epsilon

for some constant
\tilde{C}>0
. Repeating the steps to derive (
G.18
) then gives

\displaystyle\sup_{K/\sqrt{n}\leq\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}f_{n,\theta}^{(3)}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}f_{n,\theta}^{(3)}(X_{s})ds\right|\mathbbm{1}_{E}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\max_{j=1,\dots,\lceil c\epsilon^{-1}n^{1/4}\rceil}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}f_{n,\theta_{j}}^{(3)}(X_{(k-1)/n})-\sqrt{n}\int_{0}^{1}f_{n,\theta_{j}}^{(3)}(X_{s})ds\right|+\tilde{C}\epsilon.

From this, the claim follows again by applying Lemma
G.1
and Markov’s inequality as it was done for
f_{n,\theta}^{(1)}
, provided this Lemma is also applicable for
f_{n,\theta}^{(3)}
. This is indeed the case, since
\int_{\mathbb{R}}f_{n}^{(3)}(x)|x|^{m}dx<\infty
for
m=1,2
,
|f_{n}^{(3)}|\leq 1
,

\|f_{n}^{(3)}\|_{L^{1}}=\int_{-\infty}^{a+\theta}\exp\left(C\sqrt{n}(x-a-\theta)\right)dx=\int_{-\infty}^{0}\exp\left(C\sqrt{n}x\right)dx=\frac{1}{C\sqrt{n}},

and by Lemma
B.1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[f_{n}^{(3)}(X_{(k-1)/n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{-\infty}^{a+\theta}\exp\left(C\sqrt{n}(y-a-\theta)\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}}{\sqrt{k-1}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\exp\left(Cy\right)\exp\left(-\frac{(y+\sqrt{n}(a+\theta-x_{0}))^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)}\right)dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}}{\sqrt{k-1}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\exp\left(Cy\right)dy\leq\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}}{\sqrt{k}},

with all bounds being independent of
\theta
. Finally, the proof of
f_{n,\theta}^{(3)}
with
A_{\theta}=[a+\theta,\infty)
works exactly the same.
∎

The next Lemma provides a moment bound for a supremum appearing in the discussion of
\Xi_{n}^{(5,3)}
in the proof of Lemma
4.1
. Note that the order
n^{3/8}\log(n)
is suboptimal, but sufficiently good for our purpose. It could be further improved by using the metric
\rho_{n}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})^{2}=n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\wedge\sqrt{n}
in the chaining argument together with subexponential tail inequalities for martingales (see
de la Peña (1999)
).

Lemma G.3
.

Define

\displaystyle M_{n}(\theta)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt-\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}\right|X_{(k-1)/n}\right].

Then for some constant
C_{\alpha,\beta}>0
we have

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0\leq\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}|M_{n}(\theta)|\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n^{3/8}\log(n).

The proof relies on a chaining argument similar to that in Subsection
4.1
. First, for
0\leq\theta^{\prime}\leq\theta
, we rewrite

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta\leq X_{k/n}\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\leq X_{k/n}\}}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}},\end{split}

(G.19)

such that all indicators on the right-hand side depend solely on one random variable. Using that
M_{n}
is a sum of martingale differences and

\displaystyle\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[(M_{n}(\theta)-M_{n}(\theta^{\prime}))^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\right].

By direct evaluation using Lemma
B.1

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{k/n}\geq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\left.\cdot\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(1+\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-x_{0})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}(k-1)/n}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}

and using boundedness of
x\mapsto(1+x^{2})^{2}\exp(-x^{2}/4)
together with Corollary
B.2
in the last step, we also derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(X_{k/n}-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}<X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+\theta\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\int_{\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}}^{\rho_{0}+\theta}\left(1+\frac{n}{2}(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}\right)^{2}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(-\frac{(y-X_{(k-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}<\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}\}}\int_{\sqrt{n}(\rho_{0}+\theta^{\prime}-X_{(k-1)/n})}^{\sqrt{n}(\rho_{0}+\theta-X_{(k-1)/n})}\left(1+\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)^{2}\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{n}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-\theta^{\prime})^{2}}{4\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|}{\sqrt{(k-1)/n}}.

Hence,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[(M_{n}(\theta)-M_{n}(\theta^{\prime}))^{2}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}n|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|.

By the same steps that were used to derive (
G.9
) and (
G.10
) applied to the set
\mathcal{T}_{n}^{0}=[0,n^{-1/4}]
, the chaining argument gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{0<\theta\leq n^{-1/4}}|M_{n}(\theta)|\right]&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{1}^{n^{3/8}}n^{3/8}u^{-1}du\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\sum_{\theta\in[0,n^{-1/4}]\cap\mathcal{T}_{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\in U_{C/n}(\theta)}\left(M_{n}(\theta)-M_{n}(\theta^{\prime})\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

(G.20)

where
\mathcal{T}_{n}
is some finite grid of
[0,n^{-1/4}]
with cardinality
\#\mathcal{T}_{n}\leq Cn^{3/4}
for some constant
C>0
