\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\cdot\left.\left(\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\leq X_{(l-1)/n}\}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}|z_{1}-z_{2}|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[B_{n}(z_{1},z_{2})\left(\sum_{l=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\leq X_{(l-1)/n}\}}\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}|z_{2}-z_{3}|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[B_{n}(z_{2},z_{3})\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+|z_{1}-z_{2}||z_{2}-z_{3}|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[B_{n}(z_{1},z_{2})B_{n}(z_{2},z_{3})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt=:T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}.

We now show that each summand is bounded by
C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{\gamma}
with
\gamma=3/2
. Then (
I.8
) is proven and the statement of the lemma then follows.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{T_{1}}
.

Explicitly writing out the expression gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}T_{1}&=C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\}}\right]\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\leq X_{(l-1)/n}\}}\right]\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\}}\right]\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt+C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\leq X_{(l-1)/n}\}}\right].\end{split}

(I.12)

We construct an upper bound for the first summand over
k,l
in this expressions, the others can be dealt with analogously. First, notice that the product of the indicators vanishes for
k=l
. This observation is essential in order to derive a bound that is a multiple of
|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{2}
rather than a multiple of
|z_{1}-z_{3}|
. Hence, assume
k<l
. Then by Lemma
B.1
and Corollary
B.2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\left.\cdot\int_{\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n}^{\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n}\sqrt{n}\exp\left(\frac{(y-X_{(l-1)/n})^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)dy\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{2}-z_{3}|}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\left.\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(l-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-z_{2}/n)^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{2}-z_{3}|}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\Bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt\left.\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left.\left[\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(l-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-z_{2}/n)^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right|\ \mathcal{F}_{k/n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{2}-z_{3}|}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{l-k}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{2}-z_{3}|}{\sqrt{n(l-k)}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left.\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\right|\ \mathcal{F}_{(k-1)/n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}||z_{2}-z_{3}|}{n\sqrt{l-k}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\}}\exp\left(-\frac{(X_{(k-1)/n}-\rho_{0}-z_{1}/n)^{2}}{2\max\{\alpha^{2},\beta^{2}\}/n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{2}}{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{l-k}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}.

This gives

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(l-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{l/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{3}/n\}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{2}}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{l-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{l-k}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{2}.

Repeating this step analogously for the other terms in (
I.12
) finally gives with
|z_{1}-z_{3}|\leq 2K
that

T_{1}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{3/2}.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{T_{2}}
.

Applying Cauchy–Schwarz’ inequality gives (using
|z_{2}-z_{3}|\leq|z_{1}-z_{3}|
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}T_{2}&\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}|z_{1}-z_{3}|\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[B_{n}(z_{1},z_{2})^{2}\right]}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{(k-1)/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{0}+z_{1}/n\leq X_{k/n}\leq\rho_{0}+z_{2}/n\leq X_{(k-1)/n}\}}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{3/2},\end{split}

where the last inequality uses the
L^{2}
-bound (
I.10
) for
B_{n}(z_{1},z_{2})
and Lemma
B.3
for the second factor.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{T_{3}}
.

This is done along the lines of
T_{2}
.

\bullet\ \boldsymbol{T_{4}}
.

Using
z_{1}\leq z_{2}\leq z_{3}
,
|z_{1}-z_{3}|\leq 2K
, Cauchy–Schwarz’ inequality and (
I.10
),

\displaystyle|z_{1}-z_{2}||z_{2}-z_{3}|\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}\left[B_{n}(z_{1},z_{2})B_{n}(z_{2},z_{3})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[B_{n}(z_{1},z_{2})^{2}]\mathbb{E}_{\rho_{0}}[B_{n}(z_{2},z_{3})^{2}]}\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(K)|z_{1}-z_{3}|^{3/2}.

∎
